Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on adaptive structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 275. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 845 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the adaptive decomposition. By minimality, the element a^275 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 845-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
